### A36 relay identification and high-power/low-mass characterization (2026-11-26)

fa0b6253 adds tools/task0034/thermal-relay-characterization.cjs, executed locally with Node; exact script length 7101 and FNV-1a c4636c28 matched commit input. Reproduce: node tools/task0034/thermal-relay-characterization.cjs. This is a separate illustrative two-mass plant with lagged 0.1 C quantized sensor, 25ms Euler integration, 100ms relay observations, ambient 25 C, targets 200/90 C, eight cycles; common relay sequence and stability/Ku/Tu/gain rules mirrored from actual TemperatureControl.cpp and Extruder.h. J0/J1 and J2/J3 differ in gains, not the heat/cool experiment; compute both from each converged trace. Time checks sampled rather than continuously checked as actual firmware, floating point seconds/double not AVR millis/float, no actual HAL grant, ADC, AC phase, faults, airflow changes or spatial thermal gradients. Power is averaged from commanded PWM, not measured current. Grid is hypothetical, not E3D or other product specifications: hotend 30/60/100W, body 2/6/18 J/K, heater .75 J/K, coupling 4/12 W/K, loss .04/.07 W/K, sensor tau .15/.75/1.5s, cap 128/255; bed 300/450/600W, body 300/800 J/K, heater 40 J/K, coupling 8/20 W/K, loss 2/4 W/K, tau 1/5s, cap 128/255. No device calibration.

312 simulated plants; 234 converged (167/216 hotend, 67/96 bed), 78 rejected, nine hit the existing relative autotune cutoff (2 at 60W, 7 at 100W); others fail convergence, phase timeout or no-rise. Valid gain bounds checked for both allowed methods on every converged trace. At 30/60/100W converged 53/62/52 of 72 each; maximum observed 1s rise ranges 1.6..10.7/1.6..21.5/2.6..35.8 C, max off rebound 0..7.7/.1..16.5/.3..18.2 C, peak delay 0..2.1/.2..2.9/.2..4s. At 300/450/600W beds 15/24/28 of 32 each converged; rise .4...9/.3..1.3/.4..1.7 C/s, rebound 0..2.1/.1..5/0..8.1 C, peak delay 0..8.2/1.1..9.7/0..12s. Completed zero-command off phases only; interrupted/censored phase recorded separately. Example 100W/2 J/K, sensor tau .15s, coupling 12 W/K converges, cold first switch-off at 5.2s, measured rise up to 35.8 C per second; a different 100W/2 J/K plant with coupling 4 and tau .75 reaches cutoff at 6.8s, observed partial off rebound 19.3 C after 0.9s at zero commanded power. This is NOT a stuck-on indication in the model. Five representative 12.5ms replays retain outcome classification, max body difference <=.073 C, max completed rebound difference <=.3 C; not a full convergence proof.

Implication: do not use a fixed tight universal rise-rate or immediate positive slope on zero duty as fire criteria. A candidate profile needs bounded sample validity, observed rise over multiple windows, power cap and sensor identity, initial vs steady relay segments, completed off-phase maximum rebound and time-to-peak, cooling rate, variation/uncertainty, and explicit unavailable/censored evidence. A measured maximum alone is NOT a safe limit. Keep absolute measured-temperature cutoff independent and evaluate distinguishable excess/off-rise faults with uncertainty, different targets, hot/cold starts, fan/filament and cross-heater disturbances, sensor detachment/frozen and failed-closed actuator. Compare adaptive bounded relay power for fast plants without weakening existing cutoffs; do not silently change configured PID power cap or store a profile after aborted tune. Next task-private actual-source streaming characterization and replay, then validate bounded profile policy with held-out plants/faults and separately plan storage migration, invalidation and independent shutdown. No new firmware cutoff, no EEPROM profile storage, no FIRE WARNING on arbitrary autotune abort.

### A38 observational policy holdout sweep (2026-11-26)

8039d8b6 adds tools/task0034/thermal-profile-validation.cpp, exact local Git blob 17d1519adc054bbc6b871fb892d6eb693ad30f60. Reproduce from checkout: c++ -std=c++11 -O2 -Wall -Wextra -Werror tools/task0034/thermal-profile-validation.cpp -o /tmp/profile-validation; /tmp/profile-validation 4; /tmp/profile-validation 8. Same 312 hypothetical parameter grid as A36. 25ms Euler: 234 converged (167 hotend/67 bed, same as A36); 12.5ms: 240 (173/67), so convergence classification is discretization-sensitive (six more hotends converge), not a full numerical convergence proof. 25ms profile masks: hotend 3:3, 7:5, 19:40, 23:44, 31:6, 51:24, 55:45; bed 31:5, 55:3, 63:59. Missing windows are common, not zero measurements. In particular no hotend profile has Later5 in this grid. Use maximum of available first/later rate for each measured window, no invalid PWM linear scaling; reset nonoverlapping windows at zero/positive command transition. Shadow candidate tuples (factor, additive C/s margin, rebound C margin, peak grace ms): (1.1,.1,.5,1000), (1.5,.3,2,3000), (2,.5,5,5000). Excess rise on measured 1s/5s window; off alarm on excess rebound after 1s or new 5s positive rise after observed peak delay + grace. One observation trips model shadow, no debounce/latch/actuator feedback. Thresholds illustrative, not selected. Independent measured cutoff 280/180 C in model only.

Each converged plant crossed with both allowed J methods and eight scenarios: cold; warm start at target-15 with changed target 220/100 C; ambient 15 C, changed target 180/70, 3/35W cooling transient and +/-0.1C deterministic perturbation; ambient 35 C, after off external 8/80W body heat and perturbation; actuator full stuck-on at scheduled off; 1.8x physical power from start; frozen sensor after 30s; stuck-off after 30s. Observational hold 480/660s + off 120/180s. 3744 traces at 25ms, 3840 at 12.5ms, three simultaneous shadow candidates per trace. All 334/134 cold, warm and fan-load traces per scenario at 25ms settled (last 30s +/-5C) with no alarms for any candidate; not general false-positive proof. Cross-heat alarms hotend 166/160/124 of 334, bed 2/0/0 of 134, no independent cutoff crossed; external heat is not necessarily safe but cannot be attributed to own failed switch solely from sensor data. Need independent mat-local protection, power/coupling and overshoot margins based on actual weakest component, not infer physical safety from bed sensor alone. Keep proposed 150 firmware ceiling/120 adjustable default candidates only, no firmware/EEPROM changes. Further sweep lower component-specific thresholds if requested. No hardware testing, flash or merge.


### Owner continuation after A48 — robust regulation before relaxing diagnostics (2026-11-26)

Owner reports historical Tyreus-Luyben tuning gave nearly overshoot-free regulation; earlier discontinuous sensor conversion tables had degraded control before corrections. Strong external fan at high setpoints can cause both bed and hotend to lose temperature. Objective: improve disturbance rejection and near-zero overshoot rather than accept ordinary oscillation or relax fault thresholds; insufficient physical power remains distinguishable from a controller issue where evidence permits. Keep protection independent, no automatic power-limit or absolute cutoff increases. Task0021 already retains J1 Tyreus-Luyben hotend, J3 conservative bed, J0/J2 dynamic alternatives, elapsed-time PID, measurement derivative with 0.5s filter, conditional anti-windup. Retain the proven baseline and two choices per heater; compare J3 against actual Tyreus-Luyben for beds as a research candidate, no new public method IDs or gain migration without evidence.

Concrete fidelity finding: TemperatureControl.cpp updateTempControlVars() bounds I contribution to min(pidDriveMax,pidMax), negative bound -min(pidDriveMin,pidMax). RF1000/RF2000 board bed defaults drive max/min 80/5, pidMax 255; default hotend profiles 120 or 130/30 with pidMax 255. In contrast A38 Pid in thermal-profile-validation.cpp clamps I to [-20,cap] and uses ideal 100ms sample and double precision, target off at <40 vs firmware MAX_ROOM_TEMPERATURE 33. A42/A45/A48 share that approximation. Prior results remain valid for those stated hypothetical controllers, not exact current defaults; do not use them to qualify margins or infer actual disturbance rejection. At steady target with zero slope/error, output equals I state; if equilibrium requires more duty than integral ceiling but less than total PWM cap, existing controller must retain positive error or cannot settle at target. Investigate this before changing tuning rules or decouple corridor. Replay actual extracted controller with real default and configured limits, quantized ADC conversion, time jitter, float semantics and actual PWM; compare full-cap conditional anti-windup or bounded back-calculation and optional bounded load/feedforward as research. No automatic increase of physical pidMax. At full admitted duty with persistent temperature deficit, classify inability to maintain heat as diagnostic evidence, not proof of detached sensor or fire, and keep existing safety response until a replacement is validated. Compare controllable vs actuator-saturated load, abrupt load removal (fan off), high-flow filament, ambient/target variations, cold/warm start and target steps. Measure peak overshoot, settling, integrated absolute error, disturbance recovery, residual offset, time at saturation, body/mat maxima, false/late/missed faults and AVR SRAM/stack/cycles. No controller can recover an operating point above available power.

Sensor fidelity: Task0007 exhaustive 2,097,152 conversion replay was bit-equivalence to starting implementation, not a proof of monotonicity/physical accuracy; audit all supported table segments and interpolated ADC codes for monotonic direction, duplicate/raw endpoints, discontinuity/slope anomalies, board-specific correction continuity and numerical D-term effects. Preserve intentional calibration unless evidence supports specific corrections; never silently smooth away electrical fault signatures. Physical accuracy/calibration and high-gradient heater behavior remain unverified. Simulation-first; no hardware tests, flash, merge, or runtime changes in this documentation checkpoint. Prioritize outstanding combined AVR link verification and approved M206 allowlist/bed limit work before production controller changes.

### A55 owner historical hardware reference and legacy PID semantics (2026-11-26)

Owner recalls extensive real-printer comparisons of heat-up screenshots and manually varied parameters: preferred curve nearly straight rising to target, small knee, then straight stable hold; Pessen for hotend and Tyreus-Luyben for bed were empirically selected. Historical I ceiling was deliberately used to suppress overshoot; too small a ceiling caused steady offset. Hypothesis: an approach-phase-only I ceiling, relaxed close to the target, could preserve both behaviors. Owner suspects changes in autotune amplitude/gain normalization and PID implementation contribute to higher synthetic overshoot; wants historical methods retained as reference and final precise coefficients/method selection deferred to hardware curves in Repetier-Host. Keep existing research models and provisional A54 selection unchanged; further simulations are hypotheses, not a basis to override hardware experience. High hotend temperature changes radiation/losses; compare tunes at low and high operating points without implying low-temperature tunes are valid everywhere. External fan effect may be real; silicone sock/airflow isolation is a mechanical option, not a verified historical fix. rf1000.de historical discussions/screenshots not recovered. No hardware runs authorized in this step.

Read-only comparison against community_development 49a60a97eefb5dbd5b5eeea4344f090979c2d070, Extruder.cpp blob 37964c9e92c22dfb4f6acd1ffd9e50fc32c400e5 and matching Configuration.h/RF1000.h/RF2000.h. Source explicitly labels old J1 Pessen dynamic++ for fast hotend and J4 Tyreus-Luyben lazy-- for heated bed. Old controller uses PID_CONTROL_RANGE=30 K: error >30 full pidMax and clears I; error <-30 off and clears I; inside integrates error each nominal 100ms, 1.5s historical measurement derivative (16-slot ring), no conditional integration. SCALE_PID_TO_MAX=0. I contribution limits are pidDriveMax (factor 10 times 0.1s) and -pidDriveMin/10 (factor -1 times 0.1s), not the modern -pidDriveMin. Configuration explicitly comments negative I and drive max 100/min 5 improve stability. Old saved autotune writes Kp/Ki/Kd only, retaining drive limits; old defaults hotend 130/120/120 max, 30 min, bed 80/5. Modern stored autotune sets full pidMax and min(20,pidMax), uses actual elapsed time, filtered derivative 0.5s, conditional integration, no hard +/-30K PID band. The old and new closed loops thus differ even with identical nominal coefficients.

Old relay bias/d updated BEFORE evaluation of preceding extrema, old Ku=4*d/(pi*(maxTemp-minTemp)), with d half the symmetric high-low duty difference. For ideal symmetric relay and identical span/actual amplitude, new full-power expression 4*cap*sin(pi*fraction)/(pi*span) at fraction=.5 is twice old result if d=cap/2. This is a normalization comparison only; old variable bias/d, integer rounding, min high/low times (hotend 2.5/5s, bed 1.5/3s), extrema resets and new convergence/hysteresis all affect measured span/Tu. No universal doubling claim, no blind half-gain patch. Compare fixed Ku/Tu scaling and old controller semantics separately in research, and later replay identical actual recorded traces through both estimators. Preserve prior source unchanged. Final hardware comparison should capture target/measured/PWM/P/I/D, calibration method/Ku/Tu and limits, hotend type, fan and insulation, test temperature; compare cold/warm heatup, hold, fan-on/off and target steps with unchanged independent thermal protections. No final parameter choice, safety relaxation, hardware test or merge.

### Owner refinement — 32-byte feature slots and write-frequency audit (2026-11-27)

Avoid universal wear leveling; measure hottest shared master/intent metadata and choose simple fixed/redundant or small ring based on evidence. Audit origin: A71b commit 22b23f6290612eb9a07471825683ae76d83b91a2 introduced float-only inRange used for 14 integral callsites. A70r removed conversion but still promoted all ranges to int32. Owner explicitly requests audit and immediate corrections. Reviewed layout, codec, patch, host whitelist/decode, validation, defaults, storage/integrity, recovery, session/byte IO, AVR adapter and live/runtime bindings. uint8_t IDs/lengths/PWM/current/modes, uint16_t physical addresses/CRC16, int16_t signed DMS limits, uint32_t 23-bit block masks/29-bit coverage mask/CRC32/rollover clocks, full signed int32_t unchecked host parameters and binary32 motion/PID values have justified domains. Do not shrink unchecked P/S or raw signed T before validation. Current GCode::T is a byte; M206 ASCII parser validates original full T before narrowing, binary T is a byte; checked Session API deliberately accepts signed full-width T for independent invalid-input tests. Existing EEPROM wire types/field widths and addresses unchanged. Some historic persisted values (32-bit pause limits and manual Z offset, 16-bit preheat values) have smaller currently admitted domains, but reducing them would change M205/M206 wire types or storage layout for negligible EEPROM savings and no demonstrated SRAM benefit. No schema churn in this round.

83b467f4816ce36005e06071ce04d4969494b7b8: use explicit rangeU8/U16/I8/I16, not universal int32. BlockDecoder checks byte span once and reads actual 8/16/32-bit width, fixed little-endian shifts instead of a generic uint32 accumulator/variable shift loop. Storage get32/put16/put32 and patch/session byte extraction use fixed shifts, preserve ascending byte access/short-circuit/readback and transaction order. No change to CRC algorithms, durable encoding, power-cut protocol or admission policy. Actual AVR size/timing impact pending.

e7b283c01adf609fa69252ed861ef10019fffb79: actual new validation bug: positive subnormal steps/mm could overflow reciprocal; allowed X/Y steps/mm could exceed uint16 default jog after rounding; ZMotion::configureTravel narrows steps/mm and Z overtravel into uint16 before queue wait scaling. Guard smallest normal binary32 and actual acceleration/travel products, X/Y rounded jog <65535.5, Z steps/mm and overtravel <65536 before conversion. These are numeric representation guards, not invented mechanical limits. Existing block semantics preserved for valid values, no new output or hot-path changes. Cross-block travel plus tool-offset products and legacy live mutations still need A72 preflight before admission. Permanent independent decoder oracle covers all 65536 addresses x 22 blocks x 3 widths, invalid sticky state and 65536 payload patterns. Integral bounds test compares original full-width bounds (assert no truncation) against float comparator. Five new geometry rejection cases and all default blocks checked. Local actual header replay compiled from verified previous artifact with exact reconstructed modified blobs (Codec 26c9fd840c742c5acbf17561edc5ea882a60d4e6, Patch 0c949e0bd06e15c9097a54fa60747c57cc4b1727, Session c58f2122bab33b42809986edf98c1f1cb2f269db, Storage 80201389bfb0d773d4dd7673dff368c7afc14b7f, Validation 6f640fa809a3a8d03761b89e1e19bfae0e696956; all matched connector). C++11 -O2 -Wall -Wextra -Werror for all four configured profiles: 17301504 address/width checks, 262144 payload cases, 796672 range checks, 20 geometry rejection cases and all 88 default blocks PASS. This local replay does not substitute full canonical fault/power-cut or linked AVR verification.

841be6a4263df6add7910ca30a73396c45ed7541 and 14197bf1ccf29b294d40510398ca5f142d8c466a: found historical pre-cutover bug in Extruder::applyCurrentSettings (called by LiveSettings::finishMotion): floor ratio then uint8 narrowing BEFORE existing cap15, ratio >=256 outside representable conversion, ratio <1 yields zero and subsequent division by zero. Clamp before narrowing; positive ratio below1 maps to1, >=15 maps15, negative/NaN conservatively maps15 (not a substitute for rejecting invalid settings). Preserve all representable nonzero ratios and existing cap; no ISR/timer clock algorithm changes. Actual source blob 0083a96fe639e29214d893886198a365000c9d0b verified and extracted helper local C++11 replay 327424 ratio cases plus nonfinite/invalid inputs PASS. Permanent tests/extruder-step-interval.py exercises same function. Inform Task0032 of cold-path conversion change before refreshing timing baseline; no parallel merge.

Other read-only findings for A72/Task0032, not silently changing operator semantics: M3076 legacy accepts nonzero bands across int16 range while new persisted validator uses configured +/-12500 and disallows individual zero endpoints; temporary [0,0] is intentionally nonpersistent. Align admission before live mutation and resolve explicit legacy policy; current new candidate may reject persistence after live application. Full derived geometry must also check X/Y axis length plus maximum absolute tool offsets, difference times steps/mm and signed negation (GCodeMotion::setToolOffset and Extruder::selectExtruderById); finite offset alone is insufficient. Tiny positive E calibration/acceleration and live G-code edits need complete cross-field validation; do not claim entire motion conversion audit passed from these isolated fixes. Preserve actual user workflow, no speculative new physical range or schema changes. Continue fixed baud, A72 and A73 after linked fit. ### A70at — Owner requirement: one Drive system (2026-11-28)

Binding owner decision: retain M3028; eliminate its foreground STEP generation, and ultimately eliminate all independent STEP/timing/position accounting paths. Specialized motion requests are allowed, independent Drive systems are not. Audit Z-CMP, Z-offset scan, matrix, probing and homing. Auto Setup remains future orchestration of existing autotune, endstop characterization and other calibration services; do not implement it now. Task0034 remains exclusive P0; no self-merge or Task0032 production release before the safety and full qualification gate.

#### Drive inventory and required integration contract (Task0034 -> Task0032)

* Normal print, jog and current G28: StepExecutor::performMove FOR_QUEUE/FOR_DIRECT, central Timer1. M3028 now uses FOR_DIRECT; keep typed request and result contract through all subsequent executor changes. Do not resurrect direct diagnostic stepping.
* Z-CMP: StepExecutor::stepSlowedZCompensation and ZMotion::stepCorrection already run under Timer1, but use an independent mass/wait cadence and correction ledger. Task0032's unified physical-Z scheduling is necessary to reconcile nominal, direct and correction pulses without fake nominal progress, direction ambiguity, or loss of signed debt. Preserve every reference and intentional suppression; verify actual physical pulse order, bounds, and reversal.
* Matrix and Z-offset scan share Calibration::moveZ (ScanProbe.cpp) -> ZMotion::ScanMove::step -> timedPulse. This is the remaining separate foreground Z STEP path (100 us high + 100 us low nominal; service every 16 pulses); ScanMove only suppresses CMP, it does not establish exclusive queue/direct ownership. Do not replace it with ordinary jog: scan has its own coordinate epoch and intentional homed min-switch overtravel. Migrate as a bounded central scan-axis request with its own centrally owned scan-position ledger (atomic snapshots), settled actual position, reason for termination, and absolute return-to-scan-reference request. Preserve fresh ADS1100 sample per increment, contact/release, DMS overload +/-10000, stale/failed sample handling, retries, configured scan travel/min overtravel, thermal service, and safe retreat; explicitly enforce positive Z upper limit and reject unsafe unhomed negative travel. Preserve RF1000 software upper vs RF2000 hardware upper handling, test all boards. Remove ScanMove::step/timedPulse once callers and replay migrate, not as premature deletion.
* G28 already uses queue execution but Homing.cpp writes motor reference and nominal/planner reference separately and infers completion from legacy queueRelative helpers. Require a central verified reference commit after actual contact and successful retreat/recontact; do not treat queue drain as proof of contact. Preserve tool offsets and Z release/overtravel semantics; failed or interrupted homing must not set homed. General homing may start unhomed, unlike M3028; do not bypass admission by weakening the diagnostic guard.
* Mechanical stop conflict: RF.cpp doEmergencyStop currently sets blockAll and inhibits E, then stopPrint and a special Calibration::moveZ(5mm,true). stopPrint can return early outside Printing/FilamentService, and ordinary queued/direct STEP execution does not universally gate blockAll. Scan RAII does not prohibit those paths. Before migrating emergency retreat, establish atomic central cancel of active/preparing/published requests, suppress stale pulse/target restoration, authorize only bounded positive Z retreat with upper limit, existing holding/enable semantics and thermal terminal priority. No automatic re-enable after block or second independent drive. A nested pressure/thermal stop must invalidate the outstanding caller result, not restore its previous request. This is an OPEN safety finding; not resolved by M3028 migration.
* Advance on Timer0 COMPA: specialized central E executor; preserve pulse exclusivity/lead/debt and actual timing. No caller-generated step loops. Do not move timing or physical ledger back into scan, homing, calibration or Auto Setup.

Concrete Task0032 dependencies: research/task0032-step-output/HANDOVER.md explicitly marks unified-Z counterfactual NOT production-qualified; nonblocking motor wake and stop-tail, explicit logical Z direction, coherent XYZ snapshot, packed transaction admission, Q23 and combined nominal/correction pulse timing still require integration. Task0032 must rebase these on final P0 guarded requests; the scan/emergency/homing contract above is a required integration deliverable, not optional future polish. P0 owns immediate safety/admission and release gates; coordinate before any production edits. A handoff is not safety clearance, not permission to leave a permanent foreground scan engine, and not a P0 merge waiver. If safe migration cannot be done on existing executor, sequence the prerequisite work under exclusive ownership rather than merging unqualified code. Test actual executor and scan service, not just orchestration mocks.

Remaining P0 release gates: exact final source/merge CI, all four combined recovery images, actual linked ISR/foreground feasible stack paths and global IRQ response incl. all masks and arrival bounds, justified SRAM reserve, heater fail-off invariants, mechanical stop issue above, recovery/EEPROM default cutover and obsolete implementation removal, complete post-cutover qualification. No hardware run or flash, no merge, no Task0032 release in this checkpoint.
